# HyperMesh Help page summaries — official 2022.3

This catalog summarizes 1,290/1,290 direct product Help pages mapped by its official Index Terms. Each row retains the Altair title, page metadata description or concise purpose, and direct source link. These pages include user workflows and Help topics, not only APIs. Open the source for full instructions, examples, and limitations.

| [Convert Free and Preserved Nodes into Temporary Nodes](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/free_and_preserved_nodes_convert_to_temporary_nodes_t.htm) | Use the Preserve Node panel to convert free and preserved nodes into temporary nodes, which will only be saved as long as they are marked as a temporary node. |
| [Convert Free and Temporary Nodes into Preserved Nodes](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/free_and_temporary_nodes_convert_to_preserved_nodes_t.htm) | Use the Preserve Node panel to convert free and temporary nodes into preserved nodes, which will be permanently saved in your model or a Solver deck. |
| [Ruled Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help003.htm) | Use the Ruled panel to create surfaces and/or meshes of plate elements from nodes, lines, and/or line segments, in any combination. |
| [Translate Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help004.htm) | Use the Translate panel to move entities in a single specified direction. |
| [Reflect Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help005.htm) | Use the Reflect panel to reflect portions of a model about a plane, changing the selected portion into a mirror image of itself. |
| [Lines Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help006.htm) | Use the Lines panel to create lines using a wide variety of methods. |
| [Organize Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help007.htm) | Use the Organize panel to (re)organize your database by copying or moving data (entities) among collectors, includes, or parts. |
| [Edit Element Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help008.htm) | Use the Edit Element panel to hand build, combine, split, or modify elements. |
| [Distance Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help009.htm) | Use the Distance panel to determine the distance between two nodes/points or the angle between three nodes/points, or to change distances or angles. |
| [Delete Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help010.htm) | Use the Delete panel to delete data from a model database, preview and delete empty collectors, and preview and delete unused property collectors, material collectors, or curves. You can also delete an entire model database, if you wish to start with a clean database. |
| [Project Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help011.htm) | Use the Project panel to project data entities to a plane, vector, surface, or line. |
| [Replace Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help012.htm) | Use the Replace panel to relocate one node to the position of another or manually equivalence two nodes. |
| [Edges Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help013.htm) | Use the Edges panel to find the free edges in a group of shell elements, find "T" connections in a group of shell elements (any edges connected to three or more elements), display duplicate nodes, and equivalence duplicate nodes. |
| [By Window](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help014.htm) | Use the By Window option in the Extended Entity Selection menu to create a window that can be used to select entities on your model. |
| [Rotate Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help015.htm) | Use the Rotate panel to rotate entities around an axis in space. |
| [Nodes Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help016.htm) | Use the Nodes panel to create nodes using a wide variety of methods. |
| [Hidden Line Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help017.htm) | Use the Hidden Line panel to create hidden line plots of the displayed structure. |
| [By Width](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help018.htm) | Use the By Width option in the Extended Entity Selection menu to select surfaces based on their width. |
| [OptiStruct Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help019.htm) | Use the OptiStruct panel to execute an OptiStruct run on your desktop. |
| [Numbers Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help020.htm) | Use the Numbers panel to display the IDs of an entity. |
| [Color Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help021.htm) | Use the Color panel to change the color attributes of a collector. |
| [Options Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help022.htm) | Use the Options panel to modify default settings in HyperMesh. |
| [Count Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help023.htm) | Use the Count panel to obtain a count of all the entities in your database. |
| [Contour Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help024.htm) | Use the Contour panel to create contour and assigned plots of your model, and view your analysis results, in either a contour or assigned plot mode. |
| [Global Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help026.htm) | Use the Global panel to control global parameters that are accessed by several different panels. These parameters remain constant until changed. It also controls which components or collectors are active. Any entities you create are stored in the active collectors. Finally, you can use this panel to specify which template file you want to use. (Template files must first be created.) |
| [Scale Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help027.htm) | Use the Scale panel to increase or decrease an entity's dimensions. |
| [Detach Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help028.htm) | Use the Detach panel to detach elements from the surrounding structure. You can detach elements from a portion of your model so that it can be translated or moved, or you can offset the new nodes by a specified value. You can also use this panel to detach and remove elements from your model. |
| [Normals Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help030.htm) | Use the Normals panel to display and reverse the normals of elements or surfaces. The orientation of element normals can also be adjusted. The normal of an element is determined by following the order of nodes of the element using the right-hand rule. |
| [Check Elems Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help031.htm) | Use the Check Elems pane to verify the basic quality of your elements, as well as the geometric qualities of those elements. |
| [Systems Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help032.htm) | Use the Systems panel to create rectangular, cylindrical and spherical coordinate systems. Use this function when you want to define nodes, loads and constraints in a different coordinate system. |
| [Forces Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help033.htm) | Use the Forces panel to create concentrated forces. This is accomplished by applying a load, representing forces, to a node, point, set, or component. |
| [Length Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help034.htm) | Use the Length panel to determine the length of a selected group of lines, and determine the length of the black line. |
| [Mask Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help035.htm) | Use the Mask panel to mask entities from the display list. |
| [Tetramesh Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help037.htm) | Use the Tetramesh panel to fill an enclosed volume with first or second order tetrahedral elements. |
| [Drag Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help038.htm) | Use the Drag panel to create a surface and/or mesh by dragging a series of nodes or lines, or to create elements by dragging selected elements. The selected entities are dragged along the specified vector creating a mesh, surface, or elements along that vector. |
| [Solid Map Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help040.htm) | Use the Solid Map panel to create a mesh of solid elements in a solid geometric volume. |
| [Velocities Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help041.htm) | Use the Velocities panel to create concentrated velocities. |
| [Accels Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help042.htm) | Use the Accels (accelerations) panel to create and update concentrated accelerations by applying a load, representing accelerations, nodes, components, sets, surfaces, points, or lines. |
| [Constraints Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help043.htm) | Use the Constraints panel to place constraints or enforced displacements on a model. This is accomplished by assigning a degree of freedom (DOF) constraint to the node. |
| [Moments Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help044.htm) | Use the Moments panel to create concentrated moments by applying a load, representing moments, to a node. |
| [On Plane Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help045.htm) | Use the On Plane panel to select entities that are on or touch a plane. |
| [Pressures Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help046.htm) | Use the Pressures panel to create pressure loads on elements by applying a load, representing pressures, to a 1D or 2D element, or to the face of a solid element. |
| [Linear Solid Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help048.htm) | Use the Linear Solid panel to create solid elements between two groups of plate elements. |
| [Safety Module](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help051.htm) | Use the Safety module to access the panels most frequently used in safety analysis. |
| [Line Edit Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help052.htm) | Use the Line Edit panel to split, join, or extend lines. |
| [Springs Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help057.htm) | Use the Springs panel to create spring elements. |
| [Rigids Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help058.htm) | Use the Rigids panel to create rigid or rigid link elements. |
| [Bars Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help060.htm) | Use the Bars panel to create, review, or update bar2 or bar3 elements. |
| [Rods Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help062.htm) | Use the Rods panel to create rod elements. |
| [Renumber Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help063.htm) | Use the Renumber panel to renumber entities. You may also enter a value by which to offset the IDs of entities. |
| [Line Drag Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help064.htm) | Use the Line Drag panel to create a two- or three-dimensional surface and/or mesh or elements by dragging nodes, lines, or elements along another line. |
| [FE Joints Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help066.htm) | Use the FE joints panel to create, review, or update joint elements. A joint element is a definition of a connection between two rigid bodies. Joint elements store a property and orientation information. |
| [Temp Nodes Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help067.htm) | Use the Temp Nodes panel to control which nodes are on the temporary node mark. |
| [Mass Calc Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help068.htm) | Use the Mass Calc panel to obtain the mass, area, and/or volume of a selected group of elements, solids, or surfaces. |
| [Deformed Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help069.htm) | Use the Deformed (deformed shape) panel to plot displacement analysis results. |
| [Permute Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help070.htm) | Use the Permute panel to switch coordinates of model entities in the global coordinate system. |
| [Summary Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help071.htm) | Use the Summary panel to obtain a summary of component element counts or element properties of the current model. |
| [Load Types Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help072.htm) | Use the Load Types panel to update load types and equations. |
| [Loads Summary Tool](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help072_2.htm) | Use the Loads Summary tool to create a summary of the loads (node-based forces or moments, and element-based pressures) applied to a model |
| [Titles Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help073.htm) | Use the Titles panel to create, edit, and move screen titles and entity tags. |
| [Features Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help075.htm) | Use the Features panel to identify geometric features of a finite element model based on the relative angle between adjacent elements. |
| [Legend Edit Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help076.htm) | Legends display the range of values for the plot. Use the Legend Edit panel to change the number of colors in the legend or the colors of the legend. You can also reverse the colors used in the legend. |
| [Elem Types Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help077.htm) | Use the Elem Types panel to select the type of element to create, and also allows you to change existing element types. |
| [True View Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help078.htm) | Use the True View panel to change the current view of your model based on a model vector or angles. |
| [Super Elems Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help079.htm) | Use the Super Elems panel to assign nodes to a super element set. You can also display the nodes that belong to a super element. |
| [Sensors Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help081.htm) | Use the Sensors panel to create and edit modify sensors. |
| [Faces Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help082.htm) | Use the Faces panel to find the free faces in a group of elements, and operates in the same manner as edges, but in 3D. It also allows you to find and delete duplicate nodes. |
| [Vector Plot Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help083.htm) | Use the Vector Plot panel to create analysis-based vector plots. |
| [Temperatures Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help085.htm) | Use the Temperatures panel to create temperature constraints. |
| [Flux Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help086.htm) | Use the Flux panel to apply concentrated fluxes to your model. This is accomplished by applying a load, representing fluxes, to element nodes. Fluxes are load config 6 and are displayed as a thick arrow labeled with the word "flux." |
| [Position Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help087.htm) | Use the Position panel to specify a new position for model entities. |
| [Elem Offset Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help088.htm) | Use the Elem Offset panel to create and modify elements by offsetting from a mesh of plate or shell elements. |
| [Interfaces Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help089.htm) | Use the Interfaces panel to create and modify interfaces. Interfaces are mainly used to define contact interactions between various parts of the model. |
| [Rename Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help092.htm) | Use the Rename panel to change the name of a specific collector or to rename types of collectors to their respective ID with an optional prefix. |
| [XY Plots Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help093.htm) | Use the XY Plots panel to access the XY Plotting panels. |
| [Grid Attribs Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help094.htm) | Use the Grid Attributes panel to edit the grid line information contained in a plot. |
| [Plot Titles Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help095.htm) | Use the Plot Titles panel to edit the title information contained in a plot. |
| [Axis Labels Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help096.htm) | Use the Axis Labels panel to edit the axis label information of the current plot or selected attributes in a group of plots. |
| [Border Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help097.htm) | Use the Border panel to edit the appearance of the border that designates the window around an xy plot. |
| [Axis Scaling Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help098.htm) | Use the Axis Scaling panel to modify plot axes. |
| [Plots Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help099.htm) | Use the Plots panel to create and manage plot collectors. |
| [Curve Attribs Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help100.htm) | Use the Curve Attributes panel to create and edit curves displayed on an xy plot. |
| [Legend Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help101.htm) | Use the Legend (xy legend) panel to edit the legend associated with an xy plot, for example you can change the font size or the location of the legend. |
| [Integrate Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help102.htm) | Use the Integrate panel to obtain the area under a curve. The area under the curve that has been integrated is shaded and the total area, based on the calculation, is given. |
| [Read Curves Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help103.htm) | Use the Read Curves panel to read in an xy data set from an ASCII file. As a result, an xy curve is created that represents that data graphically. |
| [Finite Difference Panels](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help104.htm) | Use the Finite Difference panel to access the finite difference panels. |
| [Window Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help108.htm) | Use the Window panel to control xy plot window size and placement. |
| [Results Curves Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help109.htm) | Use the Results Curves panel to create curves by extracting data from an HyperMesh binary results file. |
| [Transient Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help111.htm) | Use the Transient panel to create an animation sequence from transient results. |
| [Find Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help115.htm) | Use the Find panel to locate entities in a database. |
| [Order Change Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help119.htm) | Use the Order Change panel to change element order. |
| [Assemblies Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help124.htm) | Use the Assemblies panel to create and modify assemblies, which are collections of component and multibody collectors or other assemblies. This method of grouping component and multibody collectors is useful because once an assembly is created, it is possible to display components or multibodies by assembly, or to select entities by assembly rather than by component. |
| [Solid Mesh Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help128.htm) | Use the Solid Mesh panel to create meshes in a pentagonal or hexagonal volume defined with edge lines. |
| [Masses Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help131.htm) | Use the Masses panel to create mass elements. |
| [Config Edit Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help132.htm) | Use the Config Edit panel to make changes to the configuration of existing elements. |
| [Gaps Panel - HyperMesh](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help133.htm) | Use the Gaps panel to create gap elements. |
| [Card Image Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help135.htm) | Use the Card Image panel to edit solver specific data on a set of entities. |
| [Linear 1D Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help139.htm) | Use the Linear 1D panel to create one-dimensional elements. |
| [Query Curves Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help140.htm) | Use the Query Curves panel to determine the x and y values of points in a curve on a plot. |
| [Split Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help145.htm) | Use the Split panel to split plates or solid elements. In addition, hexa elements can also be split using a technique that moves progressively through a row of elements in the model. |
| [Dependency Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help147.htm) | Use the Dependency panel to find nodes that have their degrees of freedom removed by a constraint or MPC (multiple point constraint) more than once. By identifying and correcting such dependencies prior to solving, solution errors can be avoided. |
| [Simple Math Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help148.htm) | Use the Simple Math panel to access tool for applying mathematical operations to curves. |
| [Card Editor Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help150.htm) | Use the Card Editor panel to select entities whose card image you want to view or edit. |
| [Planes Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help152.htm) | Use the Planes panel to create a square, planar surface and/or mesh in a user-specified plane, or a surface and/or mesh bounded by planar lines. |
| [Spheres Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help153.htm) | Use the Spheres panel to create a spherical surface and/or mesh. |
| [Cones Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help154.htm) | Use the Cones panel to create an analytic, conical or cylindrical surface and/or mesh. |
| [Torus Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help155.htm) | Use the Torus panel to create an analytic, toroidal surface and/or mesh. |
| [Spline Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help156.htm) | Use the Spline panel to create a shell mesh and/or surface. A mesh and surface can be created using nodes, points, or lines. You can also use the Spline panel to create a mesh without a surface, or a surface without a mesh. |
| [Convert Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help157.htm) | Use the Convert panel to exchange data between different solver formats. |
| [Smooth Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help158.htm) | Use the Smooth panel to improve element quality in a surface-based mesh or a mesh of solid elements using one or more algorithms that adjust node positions to moderate sharp variations in size or quality in adjacent elements. |
| [Automeshing Secondary Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help159.htm) | Use the Automeshing Secondary panel to create meshes interactively on surfaces, or create meshes without a surface present. |
| [Defeature Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help163.htm) | Use the Defeature panel to find and delete pinholes, fillets on surfaces and surface edges, and duplicate surfaces. |
| [Automesh Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help164.htm) | Use the Automesh panel to generate a mesh of plate elements using surface geometry or existing shell elements to define the mesh area. |
| [Skin Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help165.htm) | Use the Skin panel to create a skin surface and/or mesh from a set of lines. |
| [Spin Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help166.htm) | Use the Spin panel to create a surface and/or mesh or elements by spinning a series of nodes, a line or lines, or a group of elements about a vector to create a circular structure. |
| [Tags Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help167.htm) | Use the Tags panel to assign names to nodes, elements, lines, surfaces, points, and solids. An entity name is then used to reference the entity across multiple versions of the same model. |
| [Surface Edit Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help169.htm) | Use the Surface Edit panel to perform a variety of surface editing, trimming, and creation functions. This panel also allows you to offset surfaces in their normal direction. |
| [Node Edit Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help170.htm) | Use the Node Edit panel to associate nodes to a point, line, or surface/solid face; move nodes along a surface; place a node at a point on a surface; remap a list of nodes to a line; or project nodes to an imaginary line passing through two nodes. |
| [MBS Planes Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help172.htm) | Use the MBS planes panel to create rectangular, planar surfaces for use in multi-body analyses. The created entity is an mbplane. |
| [Apply Result Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help173.htm) | Use the Apply Result panel to apply the result's analysis data to selected entities of your model, or create loads from the analysis data. |
| [Grid Labels Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help174.htm) | Use the Grid Labels panel to edit the grid labels on the x and y axes of a plot. It allows you to change the font and color of the grid tic marks. |
| [Control Cards Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help175.htm) | Use the Control Cards panel to set job-level, solver specific data. |
| [Rigid Walls Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help176.htm) | Use the Rigid Walls panel to create and update rigid walls. |
| [Line Mesh Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help178.htm) | Use the Line Mesh panel to create a chain of one-dimensional elements such as beams along a line. |
| [Reorder Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help179.htm) | Use the Reorder panel to change the order of named entities in database. |
| [Output Blocks Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help182.htm) | Use the Output Blocks panel to create and update blocks of entities used for output requests. |
| [Edit Curves Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help183.htm) | Use the Edit Curves panel to create new curves or edit existing ones. |
| [Optimization Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help184.htm) | Use the Optimization panel to access the most frequently used topology, topography, size, and shape optimizations panels. |
| [Topography Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help185.htm) | Use the Topography panel to define design domains for topography optimization. |
| [Objective Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help186.htm) | Use the Objective panel to define the objective function for an optimization problem. |
| [Shape Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help187.htm) | Use the Shape panel to create shape design variables and associate them to a shape for shape optimization. |
| [Load Steps Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help188.htm) | Use the Load Steps panel to create and update collections of load collectors, groups, and output blocks. |
| [RBE3 Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help194.htm) | Use the RBE3 panel to create, review, and update RBE3 elements. |
| [Animation Secondary Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help199.htm) | Use the Animation Secondary panel to control the display of your model during animation sequences. |
| [Ellipsoids Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help202.htm) | Use the Ellipsoids panel to create and modify a single or multiple ellipsoids and cylinders. |
| [HyperBeam Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help205.htm) | Use the HyperBeam panel to create beam cross-section entities that you can use to simplify complex portions of your model into simple bar elements. |
| [Load on Geom Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help206.htm) | Use the Load on Geom panel to map loads or boundary conditions from geometrical entities (loads on geometry) to the associated FE mesh entities (loads on mesh). |
| [Contactsurfs Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help207.htm) | Use the Contactsurfs panel to create and modify contactsurf entities. |
| [Perturbations Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help210.htm) | Use the Perturbations panel to setup shape optimization. |
| [HyperMorph Module](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help212.htm) | Use the HyperMorph module to alter models in useful, logical, and intuitive ways while keeping mesh distortion to a minimum. |
| [Handles Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help213.htm) | Use the Handles panel to add and edit handles, or save to or load from a file. |
| [Domains Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help214.htm) | Use the Domains panel to add and edit domains, or update the domains for a model. |
| [Morph Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help215.htm) | Use the Morph panel to morph the model and create shape entities. |
| [Map to Geom Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help216.htm) | Use the Map to Geom panel to map nodes, domains, morph volume edges, or morph volume faces in your model to a line, node list, plane, surfaces, elements, or an equation using edge domains and handles to guide the process. It also allows you to map a mesh to section lines, apply the difference between two lines or two surfaces to a mesh, offset a mesh in the normal direction, and map (or create) a mesh to a surface interpolated from a set of nodes or lines. |
| [Symmetry Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help217.htm) | Use the Symmetry panel to create symmetries that influence handles, morph volumes, domains, blocks, rwalls, and shapes. |
| [Morph Options Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help218.htm) | Use the Morph Options panel to access options that are common to many of the other HyperMorph panels, and which affect morphing behavior. Morph options determine both the algorithms used for morphing as well as how the morph is carried out by controlling features like symmetries, morph constraints, automatic smoothing and automatic element quality checks. |
| [Blocks Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help220.htm) | Use the Blocks panel to create and modify a block entity, which are commonly used in most crash solvers. |
| [Connectors Module](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help221.htm) | The Connectors module contains several connector-specific panels that can be used to create and manage connectors. |
| [Add Links Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help223.htm) | Use the Add Links panel to add link entities to existing connectors. |
| [Quality Panel (Connectors)](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help226.htm) | Use the Quality panel to check the quality of connectors and the realized FE. |
| [Unrealize Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help227.htm) | Use the Unrealize panel to remove existing welds. |
| [FE Absorb Tool](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help228.htm) | Use the FE Absorb tool to add (absorb) certain FE information into connectors. FE weld entities or specific FE group definitions can be considered. |
| [Apply Mass Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help232.htm) | Use the Apply Mass panel to create a dummy connector entity to add mass to a part, or create areas of mass to represent parts not present in the CAD or FE data. |
| [Composites Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help235.htm) | Use the Composites panel to assign and review the element material orientation of a mesh of shell and continuum shell elements, or to review the fiber direction (ply angle) of individual composite layers. |
| [Composites Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help235_2.htm) | Use the Composites panel to assign and review the element material orientation of a mesh of shell and continuum shell elements, or to review the fiber direction (ply angle) of individual composite layers. |
| [Vectors Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help236.htm) | Use the Vectors panel to create a vector entity. |
| [Equations Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help238.htm) | Use the Equations panel to create, review, and update equations. |
| [Penetration Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help240.htm) | Use the Penetration panel to check for penetrations and/or intersections of elements. After running the check, you can use additional tools to check the penetration depth and move nodes in order to fix the problem areas; both penetrations and intersections can be fixed. |
| [Solver Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help241.htm) | Use the Solver panel to run an external program from within HyperMesh or assign and run a solver on selected analysis decks. |
| [Fatigue Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help246.htm) | Use the Fatigue panel to write stress, strain, force, and moment results from finite element analysis to an external file that can then be used to set up fatigue analysis. |
| [Quality Index Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help248.htm) | Use the Quality Index panel to calculate a single value to represent the quality of the displayed shell (2D) model. |
| [Dummy Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help253.htm) | Use the Dummy Positioning / Joint dof panel to rotate the dummy assemblies or specify the position of the H-Point of a dummy assembly. The dummy database must be organized as a tree structure. |
| [Topology Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help255.htm) | Use the Topology panel to define design domains for topology optimization. |
| [Spotweld Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help256.htm) | Use the Spotweld panel to create 1D elements to connect different parts. |
| [Control Vol Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help257.htm) | Use the Control Vol panel to define control volume objects within a model. |
| [Size Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help263.htm) | Use the Size panel to define design variables and properties for size optimization. |
| [Dequations Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help264.htm) | Use the Dequations panel to create user-defined functions for responses and design properties. |
| [Desvar Link Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help265.htm) | Use the Desvar Link panel to link a design variable to other design variables. |
| [Responses Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help266.htm) | Use the Responses panel to create responses for an optimization. |
| [Dconstraints Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help267.htm) | Use the Dconstraints panel to create constraints for an optimization problem. |
| [Gaps Panel - OptiStruct](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help268.htm) | Use the Gaps panel to create gap elements. When using the OptiStruct solver interface, it allows the creation of multiple node-element gap elements. |
| [Obj Reference Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help270.htm) | Use the OBJ Reference panel to define a response and its reference values for a minmax (maxmin) optimization problem. |
| [Entity Sets Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help277.htm) | Use the Entity Sets panel to create, update, and review named sets of entities. |
| [Constr Screen Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help295.htm) | Use the Constr Screen panel to define the data for constraint screening. |
| [Opti Control Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help296.htm) | Use the Opti Control panel to define control parameters for an optimization. |
| [Table Entries Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help300.htm) | Use the Table Entries panel to specify table entries for use in equations for responses and design properties. |
| [OSSmooth Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help302.htm) | Use the Ossmooth panel to extract and import the final design geometry from OptiStruct's topology, topography and shape optimization results into HyperMesh. |
| [MBS Joints Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help304.htm) | Use the MBS joints panel to define kinematic joints between two local coordinate systems to connect two multibody collectors. The HyperMesh entity created is an mbjoint. |
| [Gauge Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help305.htm) | Use the Gauge panel to create design variables (DESVAR) and property relation (DVPREL1) cards for shell and composite laminate components (PSHELL, PCOMP, and PCOMPG) selected for size optimization. |
| [Midsurface Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help311.htm) | Use the Midsurface panel to extract the midsurface representation of a solid part or to generate a finite element shell representation of a solid geometry. |
| [Surface Transparency Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help319.htm) | Use the Surface Transparency panel to set the transparency level for the surfaces of selected components. |
| [Spherical Clipping Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help320.htm) | Use the Spherical Clipping panel to focus on specific areas of the model by displaying only the portions of a model inside a three-dimensional spherical volume, while masking everything outside the sphere. |
| [Admas Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help321.htm) | Use the Admas panel to create and update masses applied to nodes. The Adams panel supports the /ADMAS Radioss and *ELEMENT_MASS LS-DYNA element. |
| [ALE Setup Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help322.htm) | Use the ALE Setup panel to create and modify input data pertaining to the Arbitrary-Lagrangian-Eulerian. |
| [Autocleanup Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help323.htm) | Use the Autocleanup panel to perform automatic geometry cleanup and prepare your model for meshing based on the parameters set in the BatchMesher criteria file. |
| [HyperMorph Shapes Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help326.htm) | Use the HyperMorph Shapes panel to create, save, load, reposition, reflect, convert, smooth, animate, and apply morphing shapes. |
| [Morph Constraints Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help327.htm) | Use the Morph Constraints panel to create constraints that restrict the movements of nodes or force compliance with dimensional requirements during morphing. Constraints are entities and are saved with the model. |
| [Morph Volume Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help328.htm) | Use the Morph Volume panel to create, edit, save, load, convert, and delete morph volumes. |
| [Freehand Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help329.htm) | Use the Freehand panel to morph your mesh without needing domains, handles, or morph volumes. |
| [Loadsteps Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help330.htm) | Use the Loadsteps panel to create Nastran and OptiStruct loadstep entities, which directly correspond to Nastran and OptiStruct loadstep definitions.. |
| [Solids Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help331.htm) | Use the Solids panel to create solid geometry using a wide variety of methods. |
| [Bodies Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help336.htm) | Use the Bodies panel to create, review, and update group entities. |
| [Joints Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help338.htm) | Use the Joints panel to create, review and update joint elements for use in an OptiStruct Multi-Body Dynamics simulation. |
| [Point Edit Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help340.htm) | Use the Point Edit panel to perform many point-specific geometry cleanup tasks. |
| [Edge Edit Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help341.htm) | Use the Edge Edit panel to alter the connectivity status (topology) of adjacent surface edges, and stitch or split surfaces, replace fillets with corners, and suppress or eliminate redundant edges. |
| [Surfaces Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help342.htm) | Use the Surfaces panel to create surfaces using a wide variety of methods. |
| [Quick Edit Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help343.htm) | Use the Quick Edit panel to split surfaces and washers, change the category (shared, free, and so on) of edges, create or delete surfaces and points, project points, and trim fillets. |
| [Solid Edit Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help344.htm) | Use the Solid Edit panel to modify solid entities. |
| [Discrete DVS Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help345.htm) | Use the Discrete dvs panel to define a discrete design variable value table for use in size or shape optimization, which maybe be referenced in the ddval = field on the Size, Gauge or Shape panels.. |
| [Free Size Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help346.htm) | Use the Free Size panel to define design domains for free size optimization. |
| [Free Shape Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help348.htm) | Use the Free Shape panel to define design domains for free shape optimization. |
| [Material Collectors Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help350.htm) | Use the Material Collectors panel to create, review and edit collectors and card card images or dictionaries. |
| [Property Collectors Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help351.htm) | Use the Property Collectors panel to create property collectors, which are used to assign physical properties to entities. |
| [Element Cleanup Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help352.htm) | Use the Element Cleanup panel to perform automatic cleanup of 2D elements based on the element quality criteria from the Quality Index panel or a separate criteria file. |
| [Connector Comparison Tool](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help353.htm) | Use the Connector Comparison tool to compare the connectors in your model to the ones in a Master Connection File (MCF) or Master Weld File (MWF). |
| [Spot Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help354.htm) | Use the Spot panel to create connectors that represent point connectors such as spot welds. These connectors can be realized as standard or custom weld representations. |
| [Bolt Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help355.htm) | Use the Bolt panel to create connectors which will be represented as a 1D element construct. |
| [Seam Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help356.htm) | Use the Seam panel to create connectors that represent line connectors such as seam welds. These connectors can later be realized as standard or custom weld representations. |
| [Area Panel (Connectors)](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help357.htm) | Use the connector Area panel to create connectors that represent connections of larger regions, such as adhesives. These connectors can later be realized as standard or custom weld representations. |
| [Realize and Hole Detect Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help359.htm) | Use this panel to specify details about hole detection, such as dimensions and feature angles, and bolt realization, including washer layers, number of surrounding nodes, and whether or not to fill holes in 2D mesh. |
| [Connector Options Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help360.htm) | Use the Connector Options panel to set general connector options as well as options specific to spots, bolts, seams, or area connectors. |
| [Mesh Edit Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help361.htm) | When creating mesh-dependent realizations with quad transitions, the quad transition meshes can overlap and disturb each other if more than one set of connectors is created too close to each other. The Connector Imprint panel enables you to reconcile such transitions with each other and then modify the underlying mesh to match the results, creating a seamless, properly meshed final result. |
| [Load Collectors Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help364.htm) | Use the Load Collectors panel to create and organize load collectors in your model. |
| [Points Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help367.htm) | Use the Points panel to create free points using a wide variety of methods. |
| [Shrink Wrap Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help368.htm) | Use the Shrink Wrap panel to generate an enclosed volume or solid mesh, and is typically used to approximate and simplify an existing model. |
| [Vector Collectors Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help370.htm) | Use the Vectors Collectors panel to create and update vector collectors. |
| [System Collectors Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help371.htm) | Use the Systems Collectors panel to create and update system collectors. |
| [Beamsection Collectors Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help372.htm) | Use the Beamsection Collectors panel to create and update beamsection collectors. |
| [Multibody Collectors Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help373.htm) | Use the Multibody Collectors panel to generate new multibody collectors. |
| [Component Collectors Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help374.htm) | Use the Component collectors panel to create and update components. |
| [Radioss Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help376.htm) | Use the Radioss panel to execute a Radioss run on your desktop from HyperMesh. |
| [Smooth Particle Hydrodynamics Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help379.htm) | Use the Smooth Particle Hydrodynamics panel to perform SPH analysis on a component with volume, such as airbags or fuel tanks. |
| [Acoustic Cavity Mesh Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help380.htm) | Use the Acoustic Cavity Mesh panel to create a fluid volume mesh for the open-air volumes of an enclosed compartment, such as the passenger compartment of a vehicle. Structural components such as seats are modeled as separate acoustic volumes. Once generated, this mesh can be used in noise/vibration testing. |
| [Composite Size Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help383.htm) | Use the Composite Size panel to define manufacturing constraints for composite sizing optimization. |
| [Composite Shuffle Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help384.htm) | Use the Composite Shuffle panel to define manufacturing constraints for composite shuffling optimization. |
| [Markers Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help385.htm) | Use the Markers panel to create, review and update sensor entities. |
| [Ribs Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help394.htm) | Use the Ribs panel to create and modify simple ribs between two surfaces. |
| [CFD Tetramesh Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help399.htm) | Use the CFD Tetramesh panel to generate hybrid grids, containing hexa/penta/tetra elements in the boundary layer and tetra elements in the core or fare field. |
| [Midmesh Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help403.htm) | Use the Midmesh Panel to automatically generate a mesh at the midplane location, directly from the input geometry (components, elements, solids or surfaces), without first creating a midsurface. |
| [NSM Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help407.htm) | Use the NSM panel to add non-structural mass entities to a model, or modify existing ones. |
| [Dimensioning Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help409.htm) | Use the Dimensioning panel to create and manipulate dimension features. |
| [Mesh Edit Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help412.htm) | Use the Mesh Edit panel to extend a mesh to meet another mesh and form a good connection between them, or to imprint overlapping meshes so that they match one another. |
| [Preserve Node Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help420.htm) | Use the Preserve Node panel to review free, temporary, and preserved nodes, convert free and temporary nodes into preserved nodes, convert free and temporary nodes into preserved nodes, and remove preserved nodes. |
| [Rebuild Mesh Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/help433.htm) | Use the Rebuild Mesh panel to streamline the process of remeshing existing meshes to generate a new mesh with good quality and flow. |
| [Auto Midsurface Tool](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/panel_midsurface_tool_auto_midsurface_r.htm) | Use the Auto Midsurface tool to extend and modify midsurfaces. |
| [Final Edit Tools](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/panel_midsurface_tool_final_edit_r.htm) | Use the Final Edit tools repair surfaces, modify targets, and imprint surfaces after the midsurface has been extracted and you do not need to make further changes that require the midsurface to be re-extracted. |
| [Interim Edit Tools](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/panel_midsurface_tool_interim_edit_r.htm) | Use the Interim Edit tools to modify plates, base surfaces, and collapsed lines on the midsurface. |
| [Review Thickness Tool](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/panel_midsurface_tool_review_thickness_r.htm) | Use the Review Thickness tool to review the thickness of surfaces (including midsurfaces) and assign new fixed, uniform thicknesses to surfaces. |
| [Sort Tool](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/panel_midsurface_tool_sort_r.htm) | Use the Sort tool to manually sort midsurfaces after extraction. |
| [Quality Index Cleanup Tools Secondary Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/panel_quality_index_cleanup_tools_secondary_r.htm) | Use the Quality Index Cleanup Tools secondary panel to access tools that assist you in manually relocating nodes and optimizing element topology. |
| [User Control Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/panel_user_control_r.htm) | Use the User Control panel to further refine your mapping operation. |
| [Remove Preserved Nodes](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/preserved_nodes_remove_t.htm) | Use the Preserve Node panel to remove preserved nodes from your model. |
| [Review Preserved, Temporary, and Free Nodes](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/preserved_temporary_nodes_review_t.htm) | Use the Preserve Node Panel to review the preserved and temporary nodes in your model. |
| [Spotweld Log File](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/spotweld_log_file_r.htm) | A spotweld .log file is written in the directory from which HyperMesh is invoked. The .log file contains information pertaining to the failed spotweld elements. |
| [Extend Surfaces Subpanel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/subpanel_extend_surfaces_r.htm) | Use the Extend Surfaces subpanel to access several controls that affect how surfaces are identified and generated. |
| [Contour Loads Tool](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/tool_contour_loads_r.htm) | Use the Contour Loads utility to create contour plots of applied loads and boundary conditions contained within load collectors. |
| [Nastran to LS-DYNA Conversion Tool](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/panels/tool_nastran_to_dyna_conversion_r.htm) | Nastran that can be converted to LS-DYNA. |

| [FEMFAT](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/connectors/realizations/femat_r.htm) | FEMFAT spotwelds are a particular realization type that conforms to the specifications outlined in the FEMFAT fatigue software. |
| [Seam Hexa Adhesives](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/connectors/realizations/hexa_adhesive_c.htm) | This realization creates a continuous or discontinuous hexa weld with a predefined pattern. |
| [Hexa Imprint (Adhesive Contact)](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/connectors/realizations/hexa_imprint_c.htm) | Use the Hexa Imprint realization to create seam lines between Overlap and L joint shell parts. |
| [Penta Imprint (Adhesive Contact) T](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/connectors/realizations/penta_imprint_c.htm) | Use the Penta Imprint realization to create seam lines between T joint shell parts. |
| [Seam Realization](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/connectors/seam_panel_realization_methods.htm) | Overview of the seam connector realization process and methods. |
| [Seam-Quad LTB](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/connectors/seam_quad_ltb.htm) | The Seam-Quad LTB realization serves and realizes t-welds, lap-welds, and butt-welds simultaneously. |
| [Seam-Rigid LTB](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/connectors/seam_rigid_ltb.htm) | The Seam-Rigid LTB realization serves and realizes t-welds, lap-welds and butt-welds at the same time. |
| [Special Realization Types](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/connectors/special_realization_types_r.htm) | Learn about special connector realization types. |
| [Spot Realization](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/connectors/spot_panel_realization_methods.htm) | Overview of the spot connector realization process and methods. |
| [Spotweld Interface](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/connectors/spotweld_interface.htm) | The spotweld feinput translator reads weld information from an ASCII file, such as a Master Weld File. |
| [Pedestrian Impact](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/crash/pedestrian_impact_r-1.htm) | The Pedestrian Impact tool automates the vehicle marking, impactors positioning and the export of solver decks with minimal input, therefore reducing the full process lead time. |
| [Pedestrian Impact](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/crash/pedestrian_impact_r.htm) | The Pedestrian Impact tool automates the vehicle marking, impactors positioning and the export of solver decks with minimal input, therefore reducing the full process lead time. |

| [Seatbelt Browser Entities and Attributes](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/crash_and_safety/seatbelt_browser_r.htm) | Create a complete seatbelt system, with meshed fabric components and solver-related seatbelt features. |
| [Seatbelt System](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/crash_and_safety/seatbelt_browser_t.htm) | Use the Seatbelt Browser to create a complete seatbelt system. |

| [Update Upon Motion](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/crash_and_safety/update_upon_motion_t.htm) | Use the Link Dummy Mechanism to update dummy and seat motion. |
| [Accelerometers](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/entities/accelerometers_r.htm) | Accelerometer entities measure acceleration in the simulation. |

| [Ale Fsi Projection](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/entities/ale_fsi_projection_r.htm) | Ale Fsi Projection entities provide a coupling method for simulating the interaction between a Lagrangian material set (structure) and ALE material set (fluid). |
| [Ale Reference System Curve](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/entities/ale_reference_system_curve_r.htm) | Ale Reference System Curve entities defines a motion and/or a deformation prescribed for a geometric entity, where a geometric entity may be any part, part set, node set, or segment set. |

| [Plies](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/entities/plies_r.htm) | Ply entities define a FEA ply which is the FEA correlation to a physical ply. |

These methods do not affect the quality of quads, and ... |

| [Fill Holes, Gaps, Patches](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/holes_gaps_patches_fill_t.htm) | Fill holes, gaps, and patches in first order and second order elements. |

| [Import/Export Mesh Controls](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/mesh_controls_import_export_t.htm) | Mesh controls can be saved and reused across different models by exporting them to a template file (*.xml) and then importing them into a new HyperMesh session from the Mesh Controls Browser. |

| [BatchMesher Mesh Controls](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/mesh_controls_type_batchmesher_r.htm) | Model and local mesh controls for BatchMesh meshing. |
| [Edge Layer Mesh Controls](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/mesh_controls_type_edge_layer_r.htm) | Model, with edge layer and without edge layer, controls to generate layers from selected lines during surface meshing. |
| [Refinement Zone Mesh Controls](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/mesh_controls_type_refinement_zone_r.htm) | Refinement zones are used to refine mesh with a defined size. |
| [Regions](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/mesh_controls_type_regions_r.htm) | Regions store information used to facilitate and automate modeling practices and processes. |
| [Surface Meshing Mesh Controls](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/mesh_controls_type_surface_meshing_r.htm) | Model, local, refinement, and feature mesh controls for surface meshing. |
| [Volume Mesh Control Options](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/mesh_controls_type_volume_meshing_mesh_quality_check_r.htm) | Check mesh quality using tools accessed from the context menu that opens when you right-click on a Volume Mesh folder in the Mesh Controls Browser. |
| [Volume Meshing Mesh Controls](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/mesh_controls_type_volume_meshing_r.htm) | Model, local and volume selector mesh controls for volume meshing. |
| [Fuse Mesh](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/mesh_fuse_t.htm) | Use the Fuse tool to connect close proximity, overlapping and intersecting parts. |
| [Refine Mesh by Pattern](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/mesh_refine_by_pattern_t.htm) | Create a regular orthogonal mesh. |
| [Create SPH Mesh (LS-DYNA, Radioss, PAM-CRASH 2G)](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/mesh_sph_create_w_panel_sph_t.htm) | Create a SPH mesh using existing elements, components, surfaces, or solids in your model. |
| [Create SPH Mesh (Abaqus)](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/mesh_sph_create_w_sph_mesh_tool_t.htm) | Create a SPH mesh using existing components in your model. |
| [2D BL Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_2d_bl_c.htm) | 2D BL meshing is a method to create a 2D mesh with or without boundary layers on planar sections defined by sets/groups of edges defining closed loops. |
| [Acoustic Cavity Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_acoustic_cavity_c.htm) | Acoustic Cavity meshing generates a fluid volume mesh used to calculate the acoustic modes (or standing waves) inside the air spaces of a vehicle or similarly enclosed structural model. |
| [Automatic Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_automatic_r.htm) | Automatic meshing generates a mesh of plate elements according to your specifications using surface geometry or existing shell elements. |
| [Element Biasing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_element_biasing_r.htm) | The automeshing process enables you to bias the placement of nodes so that their intervals are not uniform in size. |
| [EM Lattice Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_em_lattice_c.htm) | EM Lattice meshing is a method to create an axis-parallel mesh for 2D and 1D geometry input. |
| [Gasket Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_gasket_c.htm) | Gasket elements are used to model components with a very thin thickness, with their main purpose being to act as a sealing between structural components. |
| [Mesh Generation Algorithms](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_generation_algorithems_r.htm) | The mesh generation algorithms are divided into two types: those that require the presence of a surface to provide a context of operation, or those working entirely from node and/or line data. |
| [1D Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_line_r.htm) | 1D mesh that allows accurate testing of connectors, such as bolts, and similar rod-like or bar-like objects that can be modeled as a simple line for FEA purposes. |
| [Linked Versus Locked Edges](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_linked_or_locked_edges_r.htm) | Most of the surface-less mesh generation algorithms require that some edges have exactly the same element density and biasing values as other edges. Those edges are automatically linked together so that they stay balanced. |
| [Mesh Types](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_mesh_types_r.htm) | The mesh type determines the type of elements used to create mesh during automatic meshing. |
| [Mesh Modes](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_modes_r.htm) | Mesh modes are used during automatic meshing to determine the type of meshing to perform. |
| [Parameter Settings](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_parameter_settings_r.htm) | Overview of the geometry cleanup and defeaturing parameter settings used to define things such as washer layers around holes, defeaturing pinholes and solid holes, rows of elements along fillets, and many other options. |
| [Periodic Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_periodic_c.htm) | Periodic meshing is a method to create a grid containing rotational and/or translational symmetric boundaries, for example rotating machinery. |
| [Shrink Wrap Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_shrink_wrap_c.htm) | Shrink wrap meshing is a method to create a simplified mesh of a complex model when high-precision models are not necessary, as is the case for powertrain components during crash analysis. |
| [Loose Shrink Wrap Mesh](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_shrink_wrap_loose_wrap_r.htm) | Loose wrap wraps the selected elements or components, surfaces, or solids with the target element size specified, and outputs an outer-volume mesh which approximately adheres to the original FE topology. |
| [Tight Shrink Wrap Mesh](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_shrink_wrap_tight_wrap_r.htm) | Tight wrap creates a wrapped surface mesh which adheres as closely as possible to the original FE topology representation, automatically detecting and following the surface features of the model. |
| [Smoothing Algorithms](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_smoothing_algorithems_r.htm) | Supported smoothing algorithms. |
| [Solid Map Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_solid_map_c.htm) | Solid Map meshing is a method that creates a mesh of solid elements in a solid geometric volume. |
| [SPH Element Mapping](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_sph_element_mapping_r.htm) | The generated SPH elements are mapped specific cards. |
| [SPH Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_sph_r.htm) | Smooth Particle Hydrodynamics (SPH), Finite Point Method (FPM) is a technique used to analyze bodies that do not have high cohesive forces among themselves and undergo large deformation, such as liquids and gases. |
| [2D Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_surface_r.htm) | A surface mesh or "shell mesh" represents model parts that are relatively two-dimensional, such as sheet metal or a hollow plastic cowl or case. |
| [Tetra Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_tetra_c.htm) | Tetra meshing fills an enclosed volume with first order or second order tetrahedral elements. |
| [Create Tetra Meshes with the Tetramesh Panel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_tetra_create_with_panel_t.htm) | You can specify some elements to be fixed, and others to be floatable. |
| [3D Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_volume_r.htm) | Volume mesh or "solid meshing" uses three-dimensional elements to represent fully 3D objects, such as solid parts or sheets of material that have enough thickness and surface variety that solid meshing makes more sense than 2D shell meshing. |
| [Volume Shrink Wrap Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_volume_shrink_wrap_c.htm) | Produce an all-hexa or all-tetra mesh based on the selected elements or geometry, then use the shrink wrap functionality as a quick mechanism to generate solid meshes. |
| [Voxel Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/meshing_voxel_c.htm) | Voxel meshing fills an enclosed volume with voxels (hexas) of a pre-defined size. |
| [Calculate and Assign Midmesh Thicknesses](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/midmesh_calculate_thickness_t.htm) | Calculate and assign the thickness of a midmesh from solid geometry using the Midmesh Thickness tool. |
| [Midmeshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/midmesh_generation_c.htm) | Automatically generate a mesh at the midplane location, directly from the input geometry (components, elements, solids or surfaces), without first creating a midsurface. |
| [Create Midsurface Mesh](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/midsurface_mesh_create_t.htm) | Create a midsurface mesh with a thickness from 3D geometry. |
| [Assign Thickness to Midsurface](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/midsurface_thickness_assign_t.htm) | Assign a thickness to shell elements that represent the middle surface of a solid part. |
| [Midsurface Thickness Assignment Behavior](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/midsurface_thickness_behavior_r.htm) | Common and solver interface specific behavior for assigning thicknesses to the midsurface. |
| [Example: Gauge File Format](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/midsurface_thickness_gauge_file_format_example_r.htm) | Formatting for a gauge file. |
| [Check for Element Penetrations/Intersections](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/penetration_intersection_check_t.htm) | Penetration is defined as the overlap of the material thickness of shell elements, while intersection is defined as elements that actually pass completely through one another. |
| [Criteria and Parameter Settings](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/settings_criteria_parameter_r.htm) | Configure criteria and parameter settings. |
| [Solid Mesh Optimization](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/solid_mesh_optimization_t.htm) | The Solid Mesh Optimization tool can be used to improve the quality of a tetra, hexas, and second order meshes with respect to several element criteria. |
| [Create Tetra Mesh with the Tetramesh Process Manager](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/meshing/tetramesh_process_manager_tetramesh_create_t.htm) | Navigate through the different steps necessary to create a high quality tetramesh quickly using the Process Manager. |

| [Browse Library Content](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/model_build_and_assembly/browser_library_content_r.htm) | Review library content using the Library Viewer. |
| [Manage Configurations](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/model_build_and_assembly/configuration_manage_t.htm) | Group common and unique parts/part assemblies together in part sets, and organize parts and part sets that are unique to a configuration. |
| [Manage Subsystem Configurations](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/model_build_and_assembly/manage_subsystem_configurations_c.htm) | Group unique subsystems together into subsystem sets and organize both subsystems and subsystem sets into configurations. In cases of subsystem hierarchy, only the top level subsystem can be added to a set or configuration; this will automatically take all of the hierarchy into the set/configuration. |
| [Manage Subsystem Representations](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/model_build_and_assembly/manage_subsystem_representations_t.htm) | A single subsystem can have multiple CAE representations that can be loaded into the session interchangeably. |
| [Manage Subsystem Revisions](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/model_build_and_assembly/manage_subsystem_revisions_t.htm) | Manage, control, and update Major, Study, and Library subsystem revisions using the Subsystem Library. |
| [Manage Subsystems](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/model_build_and_assembly/manage_subsystems_c.htm) | A subsystem is a CAE representation of a self-contained assembly or group of assemblies within a larger system model. For example, the body, a seat, the suspension, and the center console each make up ... |
| [Add a Material Library That Already Exists](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/model_build_and_assembly/material_library_adding_an_existing_material_library_t.htm) | Adding an existing material library is useful when another user has already created a shared library and you would like to access it. The steps are essentially the same as creating a new library, but instead of selecting an empty folder, you should instead select a folder that already contains a material library. |
| [Add Materials From a File](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/model_build_and_assembly/material_library_adding_contents_from_a_file_t.htm) | In this task you will add materials to a material library from an existing file, files, or folders. |

| [Add Materials Using Matereality](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/model_build_and_assembly/material_library_adding_materials_using_matereality_t.htm) | In this optional task, if you are using Matereality, you will learn how to add materials to the material library. |
