| [Manage Transformations with the Transformation Manager](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/model_setup/transformation_manager.htm) | Manage the definition of transformation applied on the nodes and elements in order to easily position the entity using a combination of scale, rotate, and translate and symmetry functions. |
| [Transform Elements](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/model_setup/transformation_tool.htm) | Use the Transformation Tool to translate, scale, reflect or rotate elements with multiple numbers of copies, including properties and loads, attached to them. |
| [Domains and Handles Approach](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/approach_domains_handles_c.htm) | The Domains and Handles approach involves dividing the mesh into domains containing elements or nodes and placing handles at the corners of those domains. |
| [Freehand Approach](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/approach_freehand_c.htm) | The Freehand approach involves morphing by moving the nodes directly without the need to create any HyperMesh morphing entities. |
| [Morph Volume Approach](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/approach_morph_volume_c.htm) | The Morph Volume approach involves surrounding the mesh with one or more morph volumes, which are highly deformable six-sided prisms. |
| [Connector Morphing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/connector_morphing_approach_c.htm) | Connectors defined on nodes, points, and lines can be morphed using the morph volumes approach. |
| [Global Morphing with Handle Placement](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/controlling_global_morphing_with_handle_placement.htm) | Global morphing differs from local morphing in that there are no definite boundaries between the handles that restrict their zones of influence. When you perform global morphing operations, the parts of the model that are morphed are those that lie between the handles that are moving and those that are not. |
| [Manage Handles and Domains for Solid Models](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/creating_handles_and_domains_solid_model.htm) | Create handles and domains, divide your solid model, and group, split, merge domains. |
| [Create Handles and Domains for Space Frame Model](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/creating_handles_and_domains_space_frame_model.htm) | Create a global domain and global handles at useful positions throughout the space frame. |
| [Dependent Handles](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/dependent_handles.htm) | Handle can be made dependent on one or more other handles, and then those handles can be made dependent on one or more other handles, and so on. |
| [Global Domains and Handles](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/global_domains_and_handles.htm) | Each global domain is associated with any number of global handles. Global handles will only influence the nodes contained within their associated global domains. Global domains and handles are best for making large scale shape changes to the model. |
| [Local Domains and Handles](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/local_domains_handles.htm) | Each local domain is associated with any number of local handles. Local handles will only influence nodes contained within their associated local domains. Local handles are intended to be used to make small scale, parametric changes to the model. |
| [Make Parametric Changes](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/making_parametric_changes.htm) | Dimensions such as distance and angle can be changed easily in HyperMorph. |
| [Match Mesh, Line, or Surface Data](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/matching_mesh_line_surface_data.htm) | Move the handles into positions that change the shape of the model to match the mesh or geometry data. If you are going to match a mesh you need to make sure that the mesh does not get morphed when you are moving the handles. |
| [Mirror Images using 1 Plane Symmetry](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/mirror_images_using_1_plane_symmetry_1.htm) | If your space frame is symmetric, you can create a plane of symmetry at the center of your space frame and have your morphs applied in a symmetric fashion. |
| [Morphing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/morphing.htm) | Morph the shape of your finite element model. |
| [Global Handles Morphing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/morphing_global_handles.htm) | Global handles are most effective when used to make general shape changes for a model, such as changing the basic shape of a model, stretching parts of a model, or making changes that involve the movement of many local handles. |
| [Local Domains Morphing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/morphing_on_local_domains.htm) | You can change the shape of a model with local domains and handles. |
| [Partitioning](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/partitioning.htm) | Partitioning is a method of dividing 2D domains into smaller 2D domains at logical places, such as at the edges of surfaces associated with the mesh, or where the angle between elements exceeds a certain value, or where the domain changes from flat to curved. |
| [Reduce 3D to 1D using Planar Symmetry](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/reducing_3d_to_1d_using_planar_symmetry_1.htm) | Planar symmetry is similar to linear symmetry accept that it reduces two dimensions instead of one. This enables you to morph your model along a single axis with only two or more handles. |
| [Reduce 3D to 2D using Linear Symmetry](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/reducing_3d_to_2d_using_linear_symmetry_1.htm) | Use linear symmetry to apply morphs to the model in such a way that the model is essentially reduced to two dimensions. |
| [Optimization Setup](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/setting_up_optimization.htm) | Morphing can be used to create shape variables for optimization. |
| [Manage Handles and Domains for Shell Models](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/shell_model_handles_and_domains_manage_t.htm) | Create handles and domains, divide your shell model, and group, split, merge domains. |
| [Shell Model Strategies using Local Domains](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/shell_model_strategies_using_local_domains.htm) | Shell models are models that are made up primarily of shell elements, namely, quads, and trias. In general, a shell model represents many parts, each with numerous features such as holes and edges, and connected together using 1D elements such as bars and rigids. |
| [Solid Model Strategies](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/solid_model_strategies_r.htm) | Solid models are models that are made up of solid elements, namely, tetras, pentas, and hexas. In general, a solid model represents a single part with numerous features such as holes, edges, bosses, flanges and ribs. |
| [Space Frame Model Strategies using Global Domains](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/space_frame_model_strategies_using_global_domains.htm) | Space frames are models that have a sparse distribution of elements, such as a car body. Space frame models can generally have element counts in the hundreds of thousands, but their basic structure is rather simple. |
| [Symmetry Types](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/symmetry_types_r.htm) | HyperMesh supports reflective and non-reflective symmetries. |
| [Approaches to Morphing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/three_basic_approaches_to_morphing.htm) | HyperMorph utilizes exclusive HyperMesh morphing entities; domains, handles, morph constraints, morph volumes, shapes, and symmetries. |
| [Biasing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/using_biasing.htm) | Biasing allows you to control the shape of a mesh when applying handle perturbations. |
| [Morph Constraints](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/using_constraints.htm) | Morph constraints are a powerful tool that can be used to restrict the movement of nodes during morphing operations. |
| [Solid Model Display Controls](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/viewing_solid_models.htm) | The HyperMesh graphics engine supports different visual options for viewing models as you work on them. |
| [Shapes](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/morph/working_with_shapes.htm) | Shapes are collections of handle and/or node perturbations from the initial configuration of the FE mesh before the morph. |

| [FreePart](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/mvd/check_perform_freepart_t.htm) | FreePart check detects the components in a model that are not connected to any other component through ID connectors or shared nodes. |
| [Intersect](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/mvd/check_perform_intersect_t.htm) | Intersect checks provide information in Power Point reports on all the inter-part intersection/overlapping in the CAD parts. |
| [Multiple Checks](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/pre_processing/mvd/check_perform_multipleChecks_t.htm) | Multiple Checks perform multiple checks using a single interface. |

| [HM-1040: Control the Display](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_1040_controlling_the_display_c.htm) | When performing finite element modeling and analysis setup, it is important to be able to view the model from different vantage points and control the visibility of entities. Sometimes a shaded view is best, while other times, a wireframe view allows you work on details inside the model. HyperMesh has many functions to help you control the view, visibility, and display of entities. |

This is particularly useful if you are trying to obtain geometry information (surfaces) from a model containing elements only. |
| [HM-2060: Create and Edit Solid Geometry](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_2060_creating_and_editing_solid_geometry_c.htm) | The use of solid geometry is helpful when dividing a part into multiple volumes, for example, to divide a part into simple, mappable regions for hex meshing. |

The most efficient method for creating a mesh representing the part includes using the Automesh panel and creating a mesh directly on the part’s surfaces. |
| [HM: 3110: Mesh Without Surfaces](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3110_meshing_without_surfaces_c.htm) | Surfaceless meshing is defined as the creation of mesh using points, lines, and nodes rather than surfaces. Some parts may have missing surfaces and some parts may not have any surfaces at all and are instead defined by line data. Either way, a mesh still must be created. HyperMesh has a number of panels that you can use to create a mesh based on geometry rather than surfaces. |
| [HM-3120: 2D Mesh in Curved Surfaces](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3120_2d_mesh_in_curved_surfaces_c.htm) | Chordal deviation is a meshing algorithm that allows HyperMesh to automatically vary node densities and biasing along curved surface edges to gain a more accurate representation of the surface being meshed. |
| [HM-3130: QI Mesh Creation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3130_qi_mesh_creation_c.htm) | HyperMesh has a set of features designed to help you achieve good element quality more efficiently. |
| [HM-3140: Batch Meshing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3140_batch_meshing_c.htm) | BatchMesher is a tool that can perform geometry cleanup and automeshing (in batch mode) for given CAD files. BatchMesher performs a variety of geometry cleanup operations to improve the quality of the mesh created for the selected element size and type. Cleanup operations include: equivalencing of "red" free edges, fixing small surfaces (relative to the element size), and detecting features. |
| [HM-3150: Mesh a Model Using Shrink Wrap](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3150_meshing_a_model_using_shrink_wrap_c.htm) | You can use the Shrink Wrap tool to generate an enclosed volume or solid mesh. This tool is typically used to approximate and simplify an existing model. |
| [HM-3200: Tetramesh](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3200_tetrameshing_c.htm) | HyperMesh provides two methods for generating a tetrahedral element mesh. The volume tetra mesher works directly with surface or solid geometry to automatically generate a tetrahedral mesh without further interaction from you. Even with complex geometry, this method can often generate a high quality tetra mesh quickly and easily. |
| [HM-3210: Create a Hex-Penta Mesh Using Surfaces](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3210_creating_a_hex_penta_mesh_using_surfaces_c.htm) | For some analyses, it is desirable to use a mesh of hexahedral and pentahedral elements. This is especially true for parts which have a large thickness compared to the element size being used, or for parts that have many features and/or changes in thickness. Castings or forgings are good examples. |
| [HM-3220: Create a Hexahedral Mesh](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3220_creating_a_hexahedral_mesh_c.htm) | Solids are geometric entities that define a three-dimensional volume. The use of solid geometry is helpful when dividing a part into multiple volumes. For example, divide a part into simple, mappable regions to hex mesh the part. Use the Solid Map panel to create a mesh of solid elements in a solid geometric volume. |
| [HM-3270: Use the Tetramesh Process Manager](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3270_using_the_tetramesh_process_manager_c.htm) | The Process Manager contains a step-by-step checklist of procedures that allow you to quickly organize and tetramesh a geometric model. Each step is formatted in a hierarchical list that provides the order in which the process must be performed. Specialized tools are also provided at each step to simplify the process. You can perform these steps manually, but it is generally faster to perform them in the Tetramesh Process Manager. |
| [HM-3300: Check and Edit the Mesh](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3300_checking_and_editing_mesh_c.htm) | In this tutorial you will check and edit the mesh. |

| [HM-3410: Create Area Connectors](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3410_creating_area_connectors_c.htm) | Area connectors must be meshed in order to work properly. When the connector’s location is existing FE mesh elems, the connector automatically gets meshed to match the elements chosen. However, after creating an area connector on surfs, lines, or along nodes, you must use the automesh options (which display when you select one of these locations types) to create a mesh on the connector area. |
| [HM-3420: Create Bolt Connectors](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3420_creating_bolt_connectors_c.htm) | In this tutorial you will learn how to apply a bolted connection to two rear trusses. |
| [HM-3430: Part Replacement Through Connectors](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3430_part_replacement_through_connectors_c.htm) | A new part is needed in the assembly. You will also export the connector information to a single file, and then export the entire FE input deck and observe how the connector information is preserved. |
| [HM-3440: Model Build and Assembly](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_3440_model_build_and_assembly_c.htm) | This tutorial is about using the Part Browser for model building and assembly. |

A template contains solver specific formatting instructions, which HyperMesh uses to create an input file for that solver. |
| [HM-4020: Assign Properties Using HyperBeam](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_4020_assigning_properties_using_hyperbeam_c.htm) | In FEA, beams are typically modeled as 1D elements. | [HM-4040: Work with Loads on Geometry](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_4040_working_with_loads_on_geometry_c.htm) | In this tutorial, you will experiment with the export of the loads applied to geometry entities. | [HM-4070: OptiView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_4070_optiview_c.htm) | In this tutorial you will work with OptiView. |
| [Exercise 1: Kinematic Draping for a Woven Ply Modeled with Two UDs](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/tutorials/hm/hm_4090_kinematic_draping_for_a_woven_ply_modeled_with_two_uds_c.htm) | A dome is covered by a fabric. The fabric is idealized with two uni-directional plies. This method allows the material to be a simple, orthotropic, uni-directional ply level model. If the ply was modeled as a single woven ply, the material model would be anisotropic and vary spatially as the angle between the warp and weft directions changed. Nominal thickness of the uni-directional plies is 0.5 * woven thickness. |

| [QA/Model Utility Menu](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/user_interface/utility_menu_qa_model_menu_r.htm) | The QA Utility menu contains many tools to help you quickly review and clean up the quality of a pre-existing mesh. |
| [Utility Menus](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/user_interface/utility_menu_r.htm) | The Utility menu contains options that allow you to customize the standard interface to include function buttons, radio options, and text that have HyperMesh-supplied and user-defined macros associated with them. |
| [Manage Boundary Conditions](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/user_interface/utility_menu_radioss_bc_manager_r.htm) | Create boundary conditions of any load collector type other than ACTIV using the BCs Manager tool. |
| [Analyze Part Information](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/user_interface/utility_menu_radioss_part_info_t.htm) | Analyze a summary of a part’s statistics using the Part Info tool. |
| [Manage Rigid Bodies](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/user_interface/utility_menu_radioss_rbody_manager_r.htm) | Manage and review all rigid bodies in the model using the RBODY Manager. |
| [Relative Displacement](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/user_interface/utility_menu_radioss_relative_displacement_r.htm) | Create the /TH/SPRING card, which supports time histories with spring output using the Relative Displacement tool. |
| [Element Quality View](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/user_interface/view_element_quality_r.htm) | The Element Quality view allows you to investigate each specific element criteria, view a breakdown of all failed and worst elements, resolve all criteria violations at one time, and evaluate the over all quality of a mesh. |
| [Review Shell Thickness](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/user_interface/view_thickness_r.htm) | The Thickness view allows you to investigate the shell thicknesses in your model. |
| [Visualization Controls](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/user_interface/visualization_controls_r.htm) | Use the Visualization controls to change many of the visual states in HyperMesh and HyperView. |
| [Workflow Batch](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/topics/user_interface/workflow_batching_r.htm) | The Part Browser can be used to batch the model build workflow. |
